```coq
Lemma ty_apply_open_rec : forall sigma k U T,
  ty_apply sigma (open_ty_rec k U T) = open_ty_rec k (ty_apply sigma U) (ty_apply sigma T).
Proof.
  intros sigma k U T. revert k. induction T; intros k; simpl.
  - reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
  - rewrite IHT1, IHT2. reflexivity.
  - rewrite IHT. reflexivity.
Qed.

Lemma ty_apply_agree : forall sigma1 sigma2 T,
  (forall X, In X (free_ty T) -> sigma1 X = sigma2 X) ->
  ty_apply sigma1 T = ty_apply sigma2 T.
Proof.
  intros sigma1 sigma2 T. induction T; intros Hag; simpl.
  - reflexivity.
  - apply Hag. simpl. auto.
  - rewrite IHT1, IHT2; auto. 
  - rewrite IHT; auto.
Qed.
```

Hmm, the induction on T gives IHT1 : (forall X, In X (free_ty T1) -> ...) -> ty_apply sigma1 T1 = ty_apply sigma2 T1. Need to feed the restricted hypothesis: `(forall X, In X (free_ty T1) -> sigma1 X = sigma2 X)` from Hag : forall X, In X (free_ty T1 ++ free_ty T2) -> ... ✓ via `apply in_app_iff; auto` or `firstorder`. Let's write: `rewrite IHT1; [rewrite IHT2| |]; auto; intros X HX; apply Hag; apply in_app_iff; auto.` Something like that.

Then:
```coq
Lemma ty_apply_identity : forall T, ty_apply (fun X => Ty_FVar X) T = T.
Proof. induction T; simpl; congruence. Qed.

Lemma tm_apply_identity : forall t,
  tm_apply (fun X => Ty_FVar X) (fun x => tm_fvar x) t = t.
Proof.
  induction t; simpl; try reflexivity; try congruence.
  - (* tm_abs *) rewrite ty_apply_identity, IHt. reflexivity.
  - (* tm_app *) rewrite IHt1, IHt2. reflexivity.
  - (* tm_tabs *) rewrite IHt. reflexivity.
  - (* tm_tapp *) rewrite IHt, ty_apply_identity. reflexivity.
  - (* tm_choice *) rewrite IHt1, IHt2. reflexivity.
Qed.
```
Hmm, the induction will handle the cases in order: tm_bvar, tm_fvar, tm_abs, tm_app, tm_tabs, tm_tapp, tm_choice. Let me just be explicit.

```coq
Lemma tm_apply_open_tm_rec : forall sigma gamma k u t,
  tm_apply sigma gamma (open_tm_rec k u t) =
  open_tm_rec k (tm_apply sigma gamma u) (tm_apply sigma gamma t).
Proof.
  intros sigma gamma k u t. revert k. induction t; intros k; simpl.
  - destruct (Nat.eqb k n); reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
  - rewrite IHt. reflexivity.
  - rewrite IHt1, IHt2. reflexivity.
  - rewrite IHt. reflexivity.
  - rewrite IHt. reflexivity.
  - rewrite IHt1, IHt2. reflexivity.
Qed.
```
Hmm, careful with the tm_abs case: `open_tm_rec k u (tm_abs T t1) = tm_abs T (open_tm_rec (S k) u t1)`, and tm_apply gives `tm_abs (ty_apply sigma T) (tm_apply sigma gamma (open_tm_rec (S k) u t1))`. The RHS: `open_tm_rec k (tm_apply sigma gamma u) (tm_apply sigma gamma (tm_abs T t1))` = `tm_abs (ty_apply sigma T) (open_tm_rec (S k) (tm_apply sigma gamma u) (tm_apply sigma gamma t1))`. So with IHt at (S k) ✓ — since `revert k` and `intros k` makes the IH `forall k, ...` ✓.

For the tm_bvar case, both sides: tm_apply of `if k =? n then u else tm_bvar n` = if k =? n then tm_apply u else tm_bvar n ✓, and the RHS's opening: open_tm_rec k (tm_apply u) (tm_apply (tm_bvar n)) = if k =? n then tm_apply u else tm_bvar n ✓. So `destruct (Nat.eqb k n); reflexivity` ✓.

```coq
Lemma tm_apply_open_tm : forall sigma gamma t u,
  tm_apply sigma gamma (open_tm t u) = open_tm (tm_apply sigma gamma t) (tm_apply sigma gamma u).
Proof. intros. apply tm_apply_open_tm_rec. Qed.

Lemma tm_apply_open_tm_ty_rec : forall sigma gamma k U t,
  tm_apply sigma gamma (open_tm_ty_rec k U t) =
  open_tm_ty_rec k (ty_apply sigma U) (tm_apply sigma gamma t).
Proof.
  intros sigma gamma k U t. revert k. induction t; intros k; simpl.
  - reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
  - rewrite ty_apply_open_rec, IHt. reflexivity.
  - rewrite IHt1, IHt2. reflexivity.
  - rewrite IHt. reflexivity.
  - rewrite ty_apply_open_rec, IHt. reflexivity.
  - rewrite IHt1, IHt2. reflexivity.
Qed.
```
Careful with the tm_abs case: open_tm_ty_rec k U (tm_abs T t1) = tm_abs (open_ty_rec k U T) (open_tm_ty_rec k U t1); tm_apply → tm_abs (ty_apply sigma (open_ty_rec k U T)) (tm_apply sigma gamma (open_tm_ty_rec k U t1)); RHS = open_tm_ty_rec k (ty_apply sigma U) (tm_abs (ty_apply sigma T) (tm_apply sigma gamma t1)) = tm_abs (open_ty_rec k (ty_apply sigma U) (ty_apply sigma T)) (open_tm_ty_rec k (ty_apply sigma U) (tm_apply sigma gamma t1)) ✓. So rewrite ty_apply_open_rec and IHt ✓.

Let me write the definitions of VR/TR too.

```coq
(* The value interpretation: VR rho env T v means that the value v
   is in the interpretation of T, where rho interprets free type
   variables and env interprets the enclosing, de Bruijn-indexed
   type binders (innermost first). *)

Fixpoint VR (rho : atom -> Rel) (env : list Rel) (T : ty) (v : tm) : Prop :=
  match T with
  | Ty_BVar i => nth i env emp_rel v
  | Ty_FVar X => rho X v
  | Ty_Arrow T1 T2 =>
      forall a, value a /\ VR rho env T1 a ->
        exists b, (tm_app v a -->* b) /\ value b /\ VR rho env T2 b
  | Ty_All T1 =>
      forall A, lc_ty_at 0 A ->
      forall S, exists b, (tm_tapp v A -->* b) /\ value b /\ VR rho (S :: env) T1 b
  end.

Definition VRV (rho : atom -> Rel) (env : list Rel) (T : ty) (v : tm) : Prop :=
  value v /\ VR rho env T v.

Definition TR (rho : atom -> Rel) (env : list Rel) (T : ty) (t : tm) : Prop :=
  exists v, (t -->* v) /\ VRV rho env T v.
```

Note: `-->*` is a notation in scope systemf_scope, which is open ✓.

```coq
Lemma open_tm_rec_lc : forall T K d, lc_tm_at K d T ->
  forall k V, d <= k -> open_tm_rec k V T = T.
Proof.
  intros T. induction T; intros K d Hlc k V Hle; simpl.
  - destruct (Nat.eqb k n) eqn:E.
    + apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. subst n. exfalso. inversion Hlc. lia.
    + reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
  - assert (H1 : lc_tm_at K (S d) t) by (inversion Hlc; assumption).
    rewrite (IHT K (S d) H1 (S k) V ltac:(lia)). reflexivity.
  - assert (H1 : lc_tm_at K d t1) by (inversion Hlc; assumption).
    assert (H2 : lc_tm_at K d t2) by (inversion Hlc; assumption).
    rewrite (IHT1 K d H1 k V Hle), (IHT2 K d H2 k V Hle). reflexivity.
  - assert (H1 : lc_tm_at (S K) d t) by (inversion Hlc; assumption).
    rewrite (IHT (S K) d H1 k V Hle). reflexivity.
  - assert (H1 : lc_tm_at K d t) by (inversion Hlc; assumption).
    rewrite (IHT K d H1 k V Hle). reflexivity.
```

Let me add VR definitions and the env/rho agreement lemmas first, compile.

Definitions:
```coq
Fixpoint VR (rho : atom -> Rel) (env : list Rel) (T : ty) (v : tm) : Prop :=
  match T with
  | Ty_BVar i => nth i env emp_rel v
  | Ty_FVar X => rho X v
  | Ty_Arrow T1 T2 =>
      forall a, value a /\ VR rho env T1 a ->
        exists b, (tm_app v a -->* b) /\ value b /\ VR rho env T2 b
  | Ty_All T1 =>
      forall A, lc_ty_at 0 A ->
      forall S, exists b, (tm_tapp v A -->* b) /\ value b /\ VR rho (S :: env) T1 b
  end.

Definition VRV (rho : atom -> Rel) (env : list Rel) (T : ty) (v : tm) : Prop :=
  value v /\ VR rho env T v.

Definition TR (rho : atom -> Rel) (env : list Rel) (T : ty) (t : tm) : Prop :=
  exists v, (t -->* v) /\ VRV rho env T v.
```

VR_env_agree:
```coq
Lemma VR_env_agree : forall m T rho env1 env2 v,
  lc_ty_at m T ->

  VR rho env1 T v <-> VR rho env2 T v.

  - apply Hag. inversion Hlc. assumption.

  - assert (Hlc1 : lc_ty_at m T1) by (inversion Hlc; assumption).
    assert (Hlc2 : lc_ty_at m T2) by (inversion Hlc; assumption).
```

Actually, simplest: prove them by induction on l1:
```coq
Lemma nth_app_below : forall (i : nat) (l1 l2 : list Rel) (d : Rel),
  i < length l1 -> nth i (l1 ++ l2) d = nth i l1 d.
Proof.
  intros i l1. induction l1 as [| x l1 IH]; intros l2 d Hi.
  - simpl in Hi. lia.
  - destruct i as [|i]; simpl. reflexivity. apply IH. simpl in Hi. lia.
Qed.

Lemma nth_app_at : forall (l1 : list Rel) (x d : Rel),
  nth (length l1) (l1 ++ [x]) d = x.
Proof.
  intros l1. induction l1 as [| y l1 IH]; intros x d; simpl.
  - reflexivity.
  - apply IH.
Qed.
```

```coq
Lemma wf_ty_lc : forall Delta T, wf_ty Delta T -> lc_ty_at 0 T.
Proof.
  intros Delta T H. induction H as
    [Delta X Hin | Delta T1 T2 H1 IH1 H2 IH2 | L Delta T Hbody IHbody].

  - apply lc_ty_arrow; assumption.

Qed.
```

- WF_Arrow : forall Delta T1 T2, wf_ty Delta T1 -> wf_ty Delta T2 -> wf_ty Delta (Ty_Arrow T1 T2)
- WF_All : forall (L : list atom) Delta T, (forall X, ~ In X L -> wf_ty (X::Delta) (open_ty T (Ty_FVar X))) -> wf_ty Delta (Ty_All T)

```coq
Lemma lc_ty_open_inv : forall k U T, lc_ty_at k (open_ty_rec k U T) -> lc_ty_at (S k) T.
Proof.
  intros k U T. revert k. induction T as [n | X | T1 IH1 T2 IH2 | T1 IH1]; intros k H.
  - simpl in H. destruct (Nat.eqb k n) eqn:E.
    + apply lc_ty_bvar. lia.
    + apply Nat.eqb_neq in E. apply lc_ty_bvar. inversion H. lia.
  - apply lc_ty_fvar.
  - simpl in H. inversion H. apply lc_ty_arrow; [apply IH1 with (k:=k)|apply IH2 with (k:=k)]; assumption.
  - simpl in H. inversion H. apply lc_ty_all. apply IH1 with (k := S k). assumption.
Qed.
```

lc_ty_open (forward):
```coq
Lemma lc_ty_open : forall T k U, lc_ty_at (S k) T -> lc_ty_at k U -> lc_ty_at k (open_ty_rec k U T).
Proof.
  intros T. induction T as [n | X | T1 IH1 T2 IH2 | T1 IH1]; intros k U H HU; simpl.
  - destruct (Nat.eqb k n) eqn:E.
    + apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. subst n. exact HU.
    + apply lc_ty_bvar. inversion H. lia.
  - apply lc_ty_fvar.
  - inversion H. apply lc_ty_arrow; [apply IH1 with (k:=k)|apply IH2 with (k:=k)]; assumption.
  - inversion H. apply lc_ty_all. apply IH1 with (k := S k). assumption. apply lc_ty_mono with (k:=k); [lia|assumption]. 
Qed.
```

```coq
Lemma multi_trans : forall a b c, a -->* b -> b -->* c -> a -->* c.
Proof.
  intros a b c H. induction H as [x | x y z Hstep Hrest IH]; intros H2.
  - exact H2.
  - apply multi_step with (y := y); [exact Hstep | apply IH; exact H2].
Qed.
```

```coq
Lemma multi_app1 : forall t1 t1' t2, t1 -->* t1' -> locally_closed_tm t2 -> tm_app t1 t2 -->* tm_app t1' t2.
Proof.
  intros t1 t1' t2 Hs. induction Hs as [x | x y z Hstep Hrest IH]; intros Hlc.
  - apply multi_refl.
  - apply multi_step with (y := tm_app y t2).
    + apply ST_App1; assumption.
    + apply IH; assumption.
Qed.
```

```coq
Lemma multi_app2 : forall v1 t2 t2', value v1 -> t2 -->* t2' -> tm_app v1 t2 -->* tm_app v1 t2'.

  - apply multi_refl.
  - apply multi_step with (y := tm_app v1 y).
    + apply ST_App2; assumption.
    + apply IH.

  - apply multi_refl.
  - apply multi_step with (y := tm_tapp y T).
    + apply ST_TApp; assumption.
    + apply IH; assumption.
Qed.
```

```coq
Theorem fundamental : forall Delta Gamma t T,
  has_type Delta Gamma t T ->
  forall sigma rho gamma,
    (forall X, lc_ty_at 0 (sigma X)) ->
    (forall x, locally_closed_tm (gamma x)) ->
    (forall x T', lookup_context x Gamma = Some T' -> VRV rho [] T' (gamma x)) ->
    TR rho [] T (tm_apply sigma gamma t).
Proof.
  intros Delta Gamma t T H. induction H as
    [ Delta Gamma x T Hlk Hwf
    | L Delta Gamma T1 t2 T2 Hwf Hbody IHbody
    | Delta Gamma t1 t2 T1 T2 H1 IH1 H2 IH2
    | L Delta Gamma t T Hbody IHbody
    | Delta Gamma t T U Ht IHt HU
    | Delta Gamma t1 t2 T H1 IH1 H2 IH2 ];
    intros sigma rho gamma Hsig Hglc Henv.
  - (* T_Var *)
    simpl. unfold TR. exists (gamma x). split.
    + apply multi_refl.
    + apply Henv. assumption.
  - (* T_Abs *)
    ...
Qed.
```

lc_subst_tm compiles. Now the fundamental theorem and the final theorem.

Let me write the fundamental theorem carefully.

```coq
Theorem fundamental : forall Delta Gamma t T,
  has_type Delta Gamma t T ->
  forall sigma rho gamma,
    (forall X, lc_ty_at 0 (sigma X)) ->
    (forall x, locally_closed_tm (gamma x)) ->
    (forall x T', lookup_context x Gamma = Some T' -> VRV rho [] T' (gamma x)) ->
    TR rho [] T (tm_apply sigma gamma t).
Proof.
  intros Delta Gamma t T H. induction H as
    [ Delta Gamma x T Hlk Hwf
    | L Delta Gamma T1 t2 T2 Hwf Hbody IHbody
    | Delta Gamma t1 t2 T1 T2 H1 IH1 H2 IH2
    | L Delta Gamma t T Hbody IHbody
    | Delta Gamma t T U Ht IHt HU
    | Delta Gamma t1 t2 T H1 IH1 H2 IH2 ];
    intros sigma rho gamma Hsig Hglc Henv.
```
Then the 6 cases.

T_Var:
```coq
  - simpl. unfold TR. exists (gamma x). split.
    + apply multi_refl.
    + apply Henv. exact Hlk.
```

T_Abs:
```coq
  - pose (body := tm_apply sigma gamma t2).
    pose (ty1 := ty_apply sigma T1).
    assert (Hjudge : has_type Delta Gamma (tm_abs T1 t2) (Ty_Arrow T1 T2)).
    { apply T_Abs with (L := L); [exact Hwf | exact Hbody]. }
    pose proof (lc_subst_tm Delta Gamma (tm_abs T1 t2) (Ty_Arrow T1 T2) Hjudge sigma gamma Hsig Hglc) as Hlcabs.
    change (locally_closed_tm (tm_abs ty1 body)) in Hlcabs.
    unfold TR. exists (tm_abs ty1 body). split.
    + apply multi_refl.
    + split.
      * apply v_abs. exact Hlcabs.
      * intros a [Hvala Hra].
        pose (x := fresh (L ++ free_tm_tm t2)).
        destruct (fresh_notin_2 L (free_tm_tm t2) x eq_refl) as [HxL HxT].
        set (gamma' := fun y => if Nat.eqb y x then a else gamma y).
        assert (Hglc' : forall y, locally_closed_tm (gamma' y)).
        { intros y. unfold gamma'. destruct (Nat.eqb y x).
          - apply value_locally_closed. exact Hvala.
          - apply Hglc. }
        assert (Henv' : forall y T', lookup_context y ((x, T1) :: Gamma) = Some T' -> VRV rho [] T' (gamma' y)).
        { intros y T' Hlk. simpl in Hlk. destruct (Nat.eqb y x) eqn:E.
          - injection Hlk as HTeq. subst T'. unfold gamma'. rewrite E. exact (conj Hvala Hra).
          - unfold gamma'. rewrite E. apply Henv. exact Hlk. }
        destruct (IHbody x HxL sigma rho gamma' Hsig Hglc' Henv') as [b [Hstep Hvb]].
        rewrite (tm_apply_open_tm sigma gamma' Hglc') in Hstep.
        assert (Hgx : tm_apply sigma gamma' (tm_fvar x) = a).
        { simpl. unfold gamma'. rewrite Nat.eqb_refl. reflexivity. }
        rewrite Hgx in Hstep.
        assert (Hagree : tm_apply sigma gamma' t2 = body).
        { unfold body. apply tm_apply_agree.
          - intros X HX. reflexivity.
          - intros y Hy. unfold gamma'. destruct (Nat.eqb y x) eqn:E.
            + apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. subst y. exfalso. apply HxT. assumption.
            + reflexivity. }
        rewrite Hagree in Hstep.
        exists b. split.
        + apply multi_step with (y := open_tm body a).
          * apply ST_AppAbs; [exact Hlcabs | exact Hvala].
          * exact Hstep.
        + exact Hvb.
```
I need a lemma `value_locally_closed : value v -> locally_closed_tm v` — by inversion on value:

Now the final theorem:

```coq
Theorem polymorphic_identity_theorem_for_free : forall t,
  has_type [] empty t
    (Ty_All (Ty_Arrow (Ty_BVar 0) (Ty_BVar 0))) ->
  forall U v,
    wf_ty [] U ->
    value v ->
    has_type [] empty v U ->
    tm_app (tm_tapp t U) v -->* v.
Proof.
  intros t Ht U v Hwf Hval Hvty.
  pose (rho0 := fun (_ : atom) (w : tm) => False).
  pose (sigma0 := fun X => Ty_FVar X).
  pose (gamma0 := fun x => tm_fvar x).
  assert (Hsig0 : forall X, lc_ty_at 0 (sigma0 X)).
  { intro X. unfold sigma0. apply lc_ty_fvar. }
  assert (Hglc0 : forall x, locally_closed_tm (gamma0 x)).
  { intro x. unfold gamma0. apply lc_tm_fvar. }
  assert (Henv0 : forall x T', lookup_context x [] = Some T' -> VRV rho0 [] T' (gamma0 x)).
  { intros x T' Hlk. simpl in Hlk. discriminate. }
  destruct (fundamental [] [] t (Ty_All (Ty_Arrow (Ty_BVar 0) (Ty_BVar 0))) Ht sigma0 rho0 gamma0 Hsig0 Hglc0 Henv0) as [w [Hstw Hvw]].
  rewrite tm_apply_identity in Hstw.
  destruct Hvw as [Hvalw HRw].
  (* HRw : VR rho0 [] (Ty_All (Arrow (BVar 0) (BVar 0))) w *)
  assert (HUlc : lc_ty_at 0 U) by (apply (wf_ty_lc [] U Hwf)).
  pose (Sv := fun (a : tm) => a = v).
  destruct (HRw U HUl c Sv) as [b [Hstep [Hvalb HRb]]].
  (* HRb : VR rho0 [Sv] (Ty_Arrow (BVar 0) (BVar 0)) b *)
  ...
```
Wait: the type is `Ty_All (Ty_Arrow (Ty_BVar 0) (Ty_BVar 0))` — the All clause's body is `Ty_Arrow (BVar 0) (BVar 0)`; after pushing Sv, VR rho0 [Sv] (Arrow (BVar 0) (BVar 0)) b ✓.

Let me redo: multi_app2 : forall v1 t2 t2', value v1 -> t2 -->* t2' -> tm_app v1 t2 -->* tm_app v1 t2'. Here the function part must be a *value*. Is `tm_tapp w U` a value? w is a value of type ∀X.X→X, so w = tm_tabs s (values are only abs/tabs). `tm_tapp (tm_tabs s) U` is not a value (tapp isn't a value constructor). Hmm!

So the reduction `app (tm_tapp w U) v -->* app b v`: we have tm_tapp w U -->* b (Hstep). To lift under app, we use multi_app1 (which handles reducing the function part) with lc v ✓:

✓ (multi_app1 works for any t1 -->* t1'.)

```coq
Theorem polymorphic_identity_theorem_for_free : forall t,
  has_type [] empty t
    (Ty_All (Ty_Arrow (Ty_BVar 0) (Ty_BVar 0))) ->
  forall U v,
    wf_ty [] U ->
    value v ->
    has_type [] empty v U ->
    tm_app (tm_tapp t U) v -->* v.
Proof.
  intros t Ht U v Hwf Hval Hvty.
  pose (rho0 := fun (_ : atom) (w : tm) => False).
  pose (sigma0 := fun X => Ty_FVar X).
  pose (gamma0 := fun x => tm_fvar x).
  assert (Hsig0 : forall X, lc_ty_at 0 (sigma0 X)).
  { intro X. unfold sigma0. apply lc_ty_fvar. }
  assert (Hglc0 : forall x, locally_closed_tm (gamma0 x)).
  { intro x. unfold gamma0. apply lc_tm_fvar. }
  assert (Henv0 : forall x T', lookup_context x empty = Some T' -> VRV rho0 [] T' (gamma0 x)).
  { intros x T' Hlk. simpl in Hlk. discriminate. }
  destruct (fundamental_relation [] empty t (Ty_All (Ty_Arrow (Ty_BVar 0) (Ty_BVar 0))) Ht
              sigma0 rho0 gamma0 Hsig0 Hglc0 Henv0) as [w [Hstw Hvw]].
  change (t -->* w) in Hstw.
  destruct Hvw as [Hvalw HRw].
  assert (HUlc : lc_ty_at 0 U) by (apply (wf_ty_lc [] U Hwf)).
  pose (Sv := fun (a : tm) => a = v).
  destruct (HRw U HUl c Sv) as [b [Hstep [Hvalb HRb]]].
  assert (Hvv : value v /\ VR rho0 [Sv] (Ty_BVar 0) v).
  { split; [exact Hval|]. simpl. unfold Sv. reflexivity. }
  destruct (HRb v Hvv) as [c [Hstep2 [Hvalc HRc]]].
  simpl in HRc. unfold Sv in HRc.
  subst c.
  assert (Hlcv : locally_closed_tm v) by (apply value_locally_closed; exact Hval).
  apply multi_trans with (b := tm_app (tm_tapp w U) v).
  - apply multi_app1.
    + apply multi_tapp; assumption.
    + exact Hlcv.
  - apply multi_trans with (b := tm_app b v).
    + apply multi_app1; [exact Hstep | exact Hlcv].
    + exact Hstep2.
Qed.
```
